			}
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
			if p2 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "defeat" {
				if p2 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "(" {
					if NOT p2 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] "<center>defeat fire-spitter spawn in high road (0 of 3)</center>" {
						p2 tp closestmob
					} else {
						sleep .1
					}
				} else {
					sleep .1
				}
				if p2 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "collect" {
					p2 tp quest
					sleep 3
					if p2 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
						call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P2
					} else {
						p2 tp closestmob
					}
				}
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p2 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p2 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p2 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p2 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Orient_Edge_Cases
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P2
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P2..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P3
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P3..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p3 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p3 tp quest
			if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "defeat" {
				if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "(" {
					if NOT p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] "<center>defeat fire-spitter spawn in high road (0 of 3)</center>" {
						p3 tp closestmob
					} else {
						sleep .1
					}
				} else {
					sleep .1
				}
				if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "collect" {
					p3 tp quest
					sleep 3
					if p3 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
						call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P3
					} else {
						p3 tp closestmob
					}
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
			if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "defeat" {
				if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "(" {
					if NOT p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] "<center>defeat fire-spitter spawn in high road (0 of 3)</center>" {
						p3 tp closestmob
					} else {
						sleep .1
					}
				} else {
					sleep .1
				}
				if p3 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "collect" {
					p3 tp quest
					sleep 3
					if p3 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
						call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P3
					} else {
						p3 tp closestmob
					}
				}
			}
		}
		times 20 {
			if NOT p3 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {
				break
			}
		}
		if p3 loading {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "p3 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p3 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {
						print "p3 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
					}
					sleep 1
				} else {
					break
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Orient_Edge_Cases
			call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P3
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Checking X key type for P3..."
			}
		}
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Dungeons_And_Broken_Quests_For_P4
		call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
		if DebugMode = True {
			print "Checking broken quests and dungeons for P4..."
		}
		if NOT sameplace {
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Clients not in same place. Teleporting p4 to quest..."
			}
			call Close_Menus
			p4 tp quest
			if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "defeat" {
				if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "(" {
					if NOT p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] "<center>defeat fire-spitter spawn in high road (0 of 3)</center>" {
						p4 tp closestmob
					} else {
						sleep .1
					}
				} else {
					sleep .1
				}
				if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "collect" {
					p4 tp quest
					sleep 3
					if p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
						call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
					} else {
						p4 tp closestmob
					}
				}
			}
		} else {
			call Close_Menus
			call TP_All_Quest
			if DebugMode = True {
				print "Teleporting All to quest objective..."
			}
			if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "defeat" {
				if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "(" {
					if NOT p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] "<center>defeat fire-spitter spawn in high road (0 of 3)</center>" {
						p4 tp closestmob
					} else {
						sleep .1
					}
				} else {
					sleep .1
				}
				if p4 windowtext ['WorldView', 'windowHUD', 'QuestHelperHud', 'ElementWindow', '', 'txtGoalName'] contains "collect" {
					p4 tp quest
					sleep 3
					if p4 windowvisible ['WorldView', 'NPCRangeWin', 'imgBackground', 'TeamUpButton'] {
						call Check_X_Key_Type_For_P4
					} else {

					}
				}

			if NOT p4 loading {
				sleep .1
			} else {

			}
		}

				print "p4 is loading. Waiting for load to complete..."
			}
			times 30 {
				if p4 loading {
					if DebugMode = True {

					}

		call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
		call Check_If_Paused
	}
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_On_Char_Screen
	call Check_If_Paused
}
block First_Run {
	print "Through the Spiral (Arc 3)"
	print "Created by: KamarJ"
	print "Last Updated: 08/14/2026"
	print "Requirements: Fullscreen (OFF) | Resolution: 800x600 | Combat (ON) | Speedhack (OFF) | Dialogue (ON) | Un-equip all homes before starting!"
	print "Enjoy!"
}
call First_Run
call First_Run_Settings

loop {
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Watchdog_Check
	call Quest_Check_Watchdog
	if Create_Adventure_Party = True {
		if All_Accounts_Are_Questing = False {
			if Created_Party = False {
				if except p1 level > 29 {
					var Created_Party = True
					call Auto_Adventure_Party
				}
			}
		} else {
			print "Create adventure party and all questing can not both be true. Please fix and restart."
			kill
		}
	}
	call Sell_Inventory
	call Check_If_Paused
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Teleporting all clients to a safe area."
	}
	times 2 {
		tp XYZ(0,100000,0)
	}
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Checking if any account has dialogue..."
	}
	if any hasdialogue {
		times 10 {
			sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
		}
	}
	call Hub_Area_Check
	call Orient_Edge_Cases
	call Main_Quest_Handler
	call Orient_Edge_Cases
	call Check_If_Paused
	call Watchdog_Check
	call Quest_Check_Watchdog
	if DebugMode = True {
		print "Waiting preset time..."
	}
	sleep $SpeedDelay
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
	if any hasdialogue {
		times 10 {
			sameany sendkey SPACEBAR, .1
		}
	}
	call Check_If_Clients_Are_In_Combat
	call Check_If_Paused
}
